# trainer: Agent Context

[Overview](index.md) · [Agent Context](agent-context.md) · [Extracted Data](details.md) · [Network](network.md) · [RBAC](rbac.md) · [Security](security.md) · [Cache](cache.md) · [Dataflow](dataflow.md)

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- trainer is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 4 CRD(s)\: ClusterTrainingRuntime, OptimizationJob, TrainingRuntime, TrainJob.
- deploying 4 workload(s) (kubeflow-trainer-controller-manager, kubeflow-trainer-controller-manager, kubeflow-trainer-controller-manager, controller-manager) with 4 container(s).
- watching 7 resource type(s).
- with 5 webhook(s) (4 validating, 1 mutating).
- protected by 2 network policy/policies.
- connecting to external services\: grpc, s3.
- defining 6 API spec type(s) across 66 struct(s) with 192 total fields.
- with 311 operator config constants (9 image references, 4 env vars).
- following 2 reconciliation steps across 2 controller(s).
- exposing 5 Prometheus metrics.
- Python k8s client with 7 API call(s).
- integrates with management relationship via labels/annotations.
- mounts 1 ConfigMap(s) as volumes\: kubeflow-trainer-config.
